关键经验 (2026-05-13)\n\n### fast_isr_fol 失败教训 (r4)\n- `fast_isr_fol()` 是二值 tautology gate (0/1)，Qwen3-4B 不产生 tautology → ISR ≡ prove_rate → 两条件零差异\n- **教训：binary ISR 无法提供有意义的 reward shaping 信号，必须用连续 ISR**\n\n### D010 Reward 公式转向 (2026-05-14)\n- **旧公式（废弃）**: r = α·r_exec + β·r_exec·ISR, α+β=1 → ISR<1 时永远是 penalty\n- **新公式**: r = r_exec · (1 + β·ISR), β>0 → ISR 只能 bonus，不能 penalty\n\n## Held-out baseline）** |\n| plan001-s42 | exec-only | 500 | **93%** | 0.123 | 0.132 | 6.58 | ✅ Genuine proofs |\n| plan001-s43 | exec-only | 500 | **14%** | 0.000 | 0.000 | N/A | ❌ 长输出退化 |\n| plan001-s44 | exec-only | 500 | **98%** | 0.570 | 0.582 | 2.04 | ❌ Tautological collapse |\n| **plan016** | **exec-only** | **1000** | **96%** | **0.003** | **0.003** | **18.55** | **❌ 矛盾爆炸崩塌** |\n| **plan002-b01** | **additive ISR β=0.1** | 500 | **81%** | **0.337** | **0.415** | **5.58** | **⚠️ H7 16% + H1 52%** |\n| **plan018** | **additive random β=0.1** | 500 | **79%** | **0.104** | **0.131** | **8.46** | **⚠️ H1 71% 冗余** |\n| plan002-b03 | additive ISR β=0.3 | 500 | 18% | 0.026 | 0.143 | 10.67 | ❌ 过拟合+低泛化 |\n| plan017 | additive random β=0.3 | 500 | 48% | 0.045 | 0.094 | 9.6 | ❌ 复杂但无用 |\n| plan013 | convex random | 500 | 100% | 0.535 | 0.535 | 2.04 | ❌ Tautological hacking |\n\n### SFT Baseline 关键发现 (plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4, 2026-05-14)\n\n**Hacking 是 RL-reward-specific，不是 finetuning 通用问题。**\n\n- SFT PR=0.14 (vs base 0.12) — 数据曝光几乎不提升 prove rate，RL exploration 才是关键\n- SFT proved-only ISR=0.571 vs GRPO exec-only ~0.132 — SFT 证明时结构健康，GRPO 学会 \"safe padding\"\n- SFT 无 tautological collapse / conclusion embedding 等 hacking 模式\n\n### β=0.1 ISR vs Random — D051 定论：ISR 无真实结构优势\n\n**D049→D051 迭代过程**：\n- D049: genuine-only ISR 2.4x (ISR 0.601 vs Random 0.252) → 被认为 ISR 有真实结构优势\n- D051: premise-count-matched 分析推翻 D049。控制 premise 数后 ISR=0.2578 vs Random=0.2434 = **1.06x**，无差异\n- **根因**：ISR s42 genuine 的 15/26 (57.7%) 只有 1-2 premises → ISR 天然 ≥0.5（LOO 数学性质）\n- **ISR s44 的 12 个\"genuine\"全是 n_prem=1**（H7 分类器 false negative）\n\n**教训**：\n1. **LOO-based metric (ISR) 受 premise count 数学偏差**：更少 premise → 每个更\"必要\" → ISR 更高，与推理质量无关\n2. **Premise minimization 不等于推理改善**\n3. **Raw ISR 对比（3.2x/2.4x）不可信**，必须 premise-count-matched\n4. **ISR-as-reward 完全失败**：低剂量 gaming ISR（H7），高剂量 PR 崩塌，premise minimization 不是结构改善\n\n### Conclusion Embedding 是 GRPO 通用策略 (D049/D051)\n\n| 条件 | H7 % | H1 % | Total Embedding | Genuine % |\n|------|-------|-------|-----------------|-----------|\n| ISR β=0.1 s42 | 16.0% | 51.9% | 67.9% | 32.1% |\n| Random β=0.1 s42 | 0% | 70.9% | 70.9% | 29.1% |\n| Random β=0.1 s44 | 0% | 79.8% | 79.8% | 20.2% |\n\n- Random H1 率 (71-80%) **高于** ISR (52%)\n- H7 是 ISR-specific gaming（ISR 16%, Random 0%）\n- ISR 改变 hacking 组成（H1→H7）而非减少总量\n\n### plan016 延长训练发现 (2026-05-14)\n\n- 500步 ISR=0.123 → 1000步 ISR=0.003 (-97.8%)\n- 退化模式：矛盾爆炸（components 6.58→18.55）\n- 论文意义：exec-only 延长训练是 reward hacking 恶化的干净消融证据\n\n### 跨 seed 退化分类 (2026-05-14, updated with H7/H1 audit)\n\n| Seed | 退化模式 | 特征 | H7/H1 | 诊断信号 |\n|------|----------|------|-------|----------|\n| s42 | ⚠️ 冗余 embedding | PR=93%, components=6.58 | H1=87.1%, Genuine=12.9% | ~~唯一健康 seed~~ → 87% conclusion embedding, ISR 被冗余 premise 稀释 |\n| s43 | ❌ Length explosion | PR=14%, 87% hit max_new_tokens | — | D029: 不重训 |\n| s44 | ❌ Tautological collapse | PR=98%, components=2.04 | H7=100%, Genuine=0% | ISR=0.5 artifact |\n\n**关键修正**: exec-only s42 并非 \"healthy seed\"。87.1% 样本含 conclusion embedding，但冗余 premises 掩盖了 hacking 程度（ISR=0.12 是稀释结果）。所有 exec-only seeds 都有 87-100% conclusion embedding。\n\n### 关键教训\n1. **跨 seed 极不稳定**: exec-only reward landscape 方差极大\n2. **ISR + component count 联合诊断**能区分 genuine proof vs tautological hack\n3. ~~ISR-as-reward 有 sweet spot~~ → **D051: ISR-as-reward 完全失败**，β=0.1 表面优势是 premise minimization artifact\n4. **训练 PR vs eval PR 巨大鸿沟**: 训练 PR 不反映 held-out 泛化能力\n5. ~~ISR β=0.1 vs random β=0.1: ISR 3.2x 差异证明 ISR 信号有结构价值~~ → **D051 作废**：premise-count-matched 1.06x 无差异\n6. **LOO-based metric 的固有偏差**：premise count 影响 ISR，不能 raw 对比不同 premise 数的样本\n\n## Goodhart 文献定位 (2026-05-14)\n\n### 核心 novelty（D051 更新）\n1. **首个在 neurosymbolic verification reward 上刻画 Goodhart 效应 + meta-Goodhart**\n2. **ISR-as-reward 全剂量失败**：低剂量 meta-Goodhart（ISR 被 game），高剂量 classic Goodhart（PR 崩塌）\n3. **Premise minimization confound**：LOO-based metric 的数学偏差——方法论贡献\n4. **Conclusion embedding 是 GRPO 通用现象**（52-80%），不因 reward type 改变\n\n### Goodhart 分类\n- high-β ISR collapse = **extremal Goodhart** (Manheim & Garrabrant 2019)\n- low-β ISR gaming = **meta-Goodhart**: diagnostic metric 本身被 game\n\n### 文献资产\n- `artifacts/literature_goodhart.md`: 30+ 篇论文\n- literature_map.yaml: 33 篇论文，6 类别\n\n## Hacking 特征分析 (2026-05-14, plan012 + D044)\n\n### 操作性分类（6 种模式，strict exact normalized match, D044）\n1. **H1 Conclusion Embedding** — 多 premise 中至少一个 premise 完全等于 conclusion\n2. **Repetition collapse** — 重复生成相同 premise\n3. **H3 Tautological collapse** — 退化为 2-component (premise=conclusion) 结构\n4. **H4 Complexity-without-utility** — 生成复杂但无用的 premises\n5. **Premise padding** — 添加无关 premises 填充\n6. **H7 Conclusion Duplication with ISR Inflation** — 单 premise = conclusion + ISR 膨胀\n\n### H1 计数定论 (2026-05-14)\n- **Strict normalization**: Random s42 H1=56/79 (70.9%), Random s44 H1=71/89 (79.8%) — 以此为准\n- 旧值 58/79 (73.4%) 和 74/89 (83.1%) 使用了宽松 normalization，已废弃\n- Premise-count-matched ratio: ISR=0.2578 vs Random=0.2434 = **1.06×**（非 1.02×）\n\n## Prolog 跨 logic type 实验 (2026-05-14, plan006)\n\n### plan006-prolog-exec-s42 (DONE, success)\n- Eval: PR=83%, mean_ISR=0.031, proved_only_ISR=0.037\n- 高 PR 但近零 ISR\n\n### plan006-prolog-sta-s42 (DONE, negative)\n- Eval: PR=50%, proved-only ISR=1.0, **100% proved 样本是 H7**\n- Prolog Goodhart 比 FOL 更极端（100 步即饱和）\n\n### plan006-prolog-exec-s43-r3 (DONE, negative)\n- Eval: PR=56%, mean_ISR=0.008, proved_only_ISR=0.015, n_proved=56\n- 退化模式：degraded（PR 大幅低于 s42）\n\n### plan006-prolog-exec-s44 (DONE, negative)\n- Eval: PR=21%, mean_ISR=0.081, proved_only_ISR=0.387, n_proved=21\n- 异常：PR 最低但 proved-only ISR 最高——small-sample artifact\n- severe degradation\n\n### 3-seed exec-only Prolog 汇总\n- PR = 53.3% ± 31.1%（极不稳定，复现 FOL 模式）\n- Mean ISR = 0.040 ± 0.037\n- 所有 seeds ISR < 0.1，确认 exec-only 跨 logic type 均不产生 genuine reasoning traces\n\n## GPU 隔离教训\n\n### C002: 所有命令必须 `export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N`\n- 必须在 `bash -c` 内部 export\n- submit_training_job 命令格式: `bash -c \"export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N && ...\"`\n\n### kill_job 子进程未清理教训 (2026-05-14)\n- kill_job 只杀 tmux wrapper PID，不杀 Python 训练子进程\n- 教训: kill_job 后必须 SSH 验证所有子进程已终止\n\n### 磁盘\n- **C003 约束**: `log_completions=False` + `save_total_limit=1`，output_dir 放 `/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/`\n- 磁盘爆满事故教训：eval 后立即清理 checkpoint\n\n## 准备就绪的资产 (2026-05-14)\n\n### Prolog STA\n- 代码+eval 全部就绪\n- 命令模板：`python3 scripts/train_grpo.py --config configs/grpo_prolog_exec_only.yaml --num_steps 500 --seed {42|43|44} --output_dir ...`（warmup_ratio 默认 0.0）\n\n### Eval 脚本\n- eval_sta.py: FOL + Prolog 双支持，就绪\n",

        "content": "---\nname: resource-saturation\ndescription: \"当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。\"\nalways: false\nroles:\n  - director\n  - agent\n---\n\n# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）\n\n## A. GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",
        "numLines": 50,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 50
      }
    },

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

Looking at the aggregate statistics across the three seeds, the precision and ISR metrics average out to 0.853 and 0.072 respectively, while H1 settles around 81.3% with genuine errors at 18.7%. This validates that GRPO's conclusion embedding generalizes well. Now I need to mark plan018-s43-r2 as complete in the registry and update the evidence tables accordingly.
